## Text Elements

#include <stdio.h>
int main() {
    int x = 49;
    int y = 0;
    if (!(x >= 0 && x < 50 || x > 50 && x <= 100)) {
        printf("0true");
    }  else {
        printf("0false");
    }

    if (x++ == 49 || x-- == 49) {
        printf("1true");
        printf("%d\n", x); //x wird erst nach dem vergleich auf 50 erhöht
    }  else {
        printf("1false");
        printf("%d\n", x);
    }
    if (!(x >= 0 && x < 50 || x > 50 && x <= 100)) {
        printf("2true");
    }  else {
        printf("2false");
    }
    if (x && y) {
        printf("3true");
    }  else {
        printf("3false");
    }
    printf("Bitte die Zahl k eingeben: ");
    int k; scanf("%d", &k);
    if (!(k > 0 && k%10 <= 7) == (!(k > 0) || !(k%10 <= 7))) {
        printf("4true");
    }  else {
        printf("4false");
    } 

}

		{

			"strokeWidth": 0.5,

			"frameId": null,

			"isDeleted": false,
			"boundElements": null,
			"updated": 1762781106577,
			"link": null,
			"locked": false,
			"text": "\n#include <stdio.h>\nint main() {\n    int x = 49;\n    int y = 0;\n    if (!(x >= 0 && x < 50 || x > 50 && x <= 100)) {\n        printf(\"0true\");\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"0false\");\n    }\n\n    if (x++ == 49 || x-- == 49) {\n        printf(\"1true\");\n        printf(\"%d\\n\", x); //x wird erst nach dem vergleich auf 50 erhöht\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"1false\");\n        printf(\"%d\\n\", x);\n    }\n    if (!(x >= 0 && x < 50 || x > 50 && x <= 100)) {\n        printf(\"2true\");\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"2false\");\n    }\n    if (x && y) {\n        printf(\"3true\");\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"3false\");\n    }\n    printf(\"Bitte die Zahl k eingeben: \");\n    int k; scanf(\"%d\", &k);\n    if (!(k > 0 && k%10 <= 7) == (!(k > 0) || !(k%10 <= 7))) {\n        printf(\"4true\");\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"4false\");\n    } \n\n}\n\n",
			"rawText": "\n#include <stdio.h>\nint main() {\n    int x = 49;\n    int y = 0;\n    if (!(x >= 0 && x < 50 || x > 50 && x <= 100)) {\n        printf(\"0true\");\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"0false\");\n    }\n\n    if (x++ == 49 || x-- == 49) {\n        printf(\"1true\");\n        printf(\"%d\\n\", x); //x wird erst nach dem vergleich auf 50 erhöht\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"1false\");\n        printf(\"%d\\n\", x);\n    }\n    if (!(x >= 0 && x < 50 || x > 50 && x <= 100)) {\n        printf(\"2true\");\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"2false\");\n    }\n    if (x && y) {\n        printf(\"3true\");\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"3false\");\n    }\n    printf(\"Bitte die Zahl k eingeben: \");\n    int k; scanf(\"%d\", &k);\n    if (!(k > 0 && k%10 <= 7) == (!(k > 0) || !(k%10 <= 7))) {\n        printf(\"4true\");\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"4false\");\n    } \n\n}\n\n",
			"fontSize": 20,
			"fontFamily": 6,
			"textAlign": "left",
			"verticalAlign": "top",
			"containerId": null,
			"originalText": "\n#include <stdio.h>\nint main() {\n    int x = 49;\n    int y = 0;\n    if (!(x >= 0 && x < 50 || x > 50 && x <= 100)) {\n        printf(\"0true\");\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"0false\");\n    }\n\n    if (x++ == 49 || x-- == 49) {\n        printf(\"1true\");\n        printf(\"%d\\n\", x); //x wird erst nach dem vergleich auf 50 erhöht\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"1false\");\n        printf(\"%d\\n\", x);\n    }\n    if (!(x >= 0 && x < 50 || x > 50 && x <= 100)) {\n        printf(\"2true\");\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"2false\");\n    }\n    if (x && y) {\n        printf(\"3true\");\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"3false\");\n    }\n    printf(\"Bitte die Zahl k eingeben: \");\n    int k; scanf(\"%d\", &k);\n    if (!(k > 0 && k%10 <= 7) == (!(k > 0) || !(k%10 <= 7))) {\n        printf(\"4true\");\n    }  else {\n        printf(\"4false\");\n    } \n\n}\n\n",
